- **g2q**: Grill a topic or working document and emit the genuinely open ambiguities as a /question-format questions file. /question delegates its challenge here; /start-issue and /tackle-pr-comment gate plan production on it. - **ga**: Read-only Google Analytics 4 CLI (`ga`) for pulling aggregate traffic data from any GA4 property the authorized Google account can see. Wraps the GA Admin API (property discovery) and Data API (runReport / getMetadata) behind Typer, with JSON-by-default output and a --pretty Rich renderer. Use this whenever you need to analyze GA4 traffic, acquisition channels, or source attribution; break down referrers / source-medium; find top pages/posts by views; see geo or daily session trends; or run an arbitrary metric×dimension GA4 report. Triggers: "analyze traffic", "where is traffic coming from",… ([griffinwork40/ga-cli](https://github.com/griffinwork40/ga-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/ga))
- **GA Chat**: Build, index data, and run the GA chatbot locally. Knows the full startup sequence: build solution → start Aspire services → seed MongoDB → probe the chat endpoint. ([GuitarAlchemist/ga](https://github.com/GuitarAlchemist/ga/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/ga/chat) · ★2)

Use for what should the game look like, set the art direction, define the visual style. 游戏美术与创意方向。把 GAME_DESIGN 转成 ART_DIRECTION，定义可辨识的视觉身份、服务玩法的视听反馈和关键游戏时刻。用于判断游戏应该长什么样、制定游戏美术方向等需求。 ([zenstory-ai/novel-to-game](https://github.com/zenstory-ai/novel-to-game/tree/HEAD/skills/game-art-direction) · ★786)
- **game-art-direction-and-visual-development**: Use when a game needs visual pillars, shape language, colour scripting, character/world readability, cultural motif governance, 2D/3D cohesion, VFX hierarchy, an asset bible, style scalability, performance-aware art, or engine handoff. Use illustration-style-and-systems for non-game illustration sets. - **game-art-director**: Art Director (Sable Mori). Defines and defends the visual identity: art bible, shape and color language, silhouette readability, asset pipeline and budgets, asset reviews, and the AI-art policy with per-asset provenance. Use for "art style", "art bible", "color palette", "asset pipeline", "AI art policy". Not for running asset generation (use game-ai-assets). ([AlterLab-IEU/AlterLab_GameForge](https://github.com/AlterLab-IEU/AlterLab_GameForge/tree/HEAD/skills/agents/game-art-director) · ★39)
- **game-art-director**: Reviews and shapes 0→1 visual-定调期 decisions for game art direction — finding the style, defining visual DNA, shipping a v0.1 style guide, running the first concept reviews. Activates when the user is establishing a game's visual identity, writing or critiquing an art bible / style guide, doing concept-art critique, debating realism vs stylization, picking thematic keywords, or trying to escape the "Cult of Good Enough" trap. Does not handle scale-stage art ops (1→10 multi-team production lines), long-term IP maintenance (seasons, collaborations, merch derivatives), craft-level IC mentoring (s… ([PlevanTem/luban-skill](https://github.com/PlevanTem/luban-skill/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/game-art-director))
- **game-art-generation**: Generate and post-process AI image assets for the game — one-style asset sets (tiles, tree/growth sprites), image-to-image reference anchoring, per-image visual review, chroma-key + mask pipelines, and contact-sheet approval.
